+from math import hypot
+
+from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
+
+
+SRC = r"C:\Users\asali\.codex\generated_images\01a05455-f941-7b83-9f83-a6b71451313d\exec-d7d3aebe-85ce-45db-818b-893de
a4b7509.png"
+OUT = r"C:\Users\asali\.codex\generated_images\01a05455-f941-7b83-9f83-a6b71451313d\warp_test.png"
+
+
+def is_green(pixel):
+    r, g, b, _ = pixel
+    return g >= 80 and g >= r + 18 and g >= b + 18
+
+
+def make_mask(image, polygon):
+    region = Image.new("L", image.size, 0)
+    ImageDraw.Draw(region).polygon(polygon, fill=255)
+    rp = region.load()
+    ip = image.load()
+    xs = [p[0] for p in polygon]
+    ys = [p[1] for p in polygon]
+    for y in range(min(ys), max(ys) + 1):
+        for x in range(min(xs), max(xs) + 1):
+            if not rp[x, y]:
+                continue
+            pixel = ip[x, y]
+            if is_green(pixel):
+                rp[x, y] = 0
+                continue
+            r, g, b, _ = pixel
+            hi, lo = max(r, g, b), min(r, g, b)
+            # Greaves are neutral/warm silver plus near-black ink edges. This
+            # rejects the saturated blue cloak, brown hammer, and gold trim.
+            neutral_armor = hi - lo <= max(8, int(hi * 0.23))
+            if not neutral_armor:
+                rp[x, y] = 0
+    return region
+
+
+def affine_from_segments(source_a, source_b, dest_a, dest_b, width_scale=1.0):
+    sx, sy = source_b[0] - source_a[0], source_b[1] - source_a[1]
+    dx, dy = dest_b[0] - dest_a[0], dest_b[1] - dest_a[1]
+    sl = hypot(sx, sy)
+    dl = hypot(dx, dy)
+    # Source/destination orthonormal bases. Length changes along the shin,
+    # while width_scale keeps the greave from becoming unnaturally thick.
+    sux, suy = sx / sl, sy / sl
+    snx, sny = -suy, sux
+    dux, duy = dx / dl, dy / dl
+    dnx, dny = -duy * width_scale, dux * width_scale
+    along = dl / sl
+    # A = D * S^T because S is orthonormal.
+    a = dux * along * sux + dnx * snx
+    b = dux * along * suy + dnx * sny
+    d = duy * along * sux + dny * snx
+    e = duy * along * suy + dny * sny
+    tx = dest_a[0] - a * source_a[0] - b * source_a[1]
+    ty = dest_a[1] - d * source_a[0] - e * source_a[1]
+    det = a * e - b * d
+    ia, ib = e / det, -b / det
+    id_, ie = -d / det, a / det
+    ic = -(ia * tx + ib * ty)
+    iff = -(id_ * tx + ie * ty)
+    return ia, ib, ic, id_, ie, iff
+
+
+def warp(image, mask, source_a, source_b, dest_a, dest_b, width_scale=1.0):
+    layer = Image.new("RGBA", image.size, (0, 0, 0, 0))
+    layer.paste(image, (0, 0), mask)
+    coeff = affine_from_segments(source_a, source_b, dest_a, dest_b, width_scale)
+    return layer.transform(
+        image.size,
+        Image.Transform.AFFINE,
+        coeff,
+        resample=Image.Resampling.BICUBIC,
+    )
+
+
+original = Image.open(SRC).convert("RGBA")
+base = original.copy()
+
+# Tight masks around the four original greaves only. Boots, knees, skirt,
+# hammer, and shield remain in the unmodified base layer.
+f3_swing = make_mask(original, [(804, 424), (843, 423), (849, 448), (831, 477), (818, 508), (785, 509), (791, 478), 
(803, 449)])
+f3_plant = make_mask(original, [(902, 442), (932, 438), (942, 463), (953, 506), (919, 509), (909, 480)])
+f4_swing = make_mask(original, [(1136, 424), (1177, 421), (1187, 449), (1169, 478), (1154, 508), (1118, 509), (1124, 
478), (1136, 449)])
+f4_plant = make_mask(original, [(1255, 439), (1285, 438), (1293, 464), (1308, 507), (1275, 509), (1264, 480)])
+clean_greave = make_mask(original, [(197, 431), (227, 430), (234, 466), (240, 507), (205, 509), (201, 474)])
+
+for mask in (f3_swing, f3_plant, f4_swing, f4_plant):
+    base.paste((0, 255, 0, 255), (0, 0), mask)
+
+# Background planted greaves first.
+base.alpha_composite(warp(original, clean_greave, (213, 442), (224, 504), (909, 442), (805, 504), 0.72))
+base.alpha_composite(warp(original, clean_greave, (213, 442), (224, 504), (1168, 442), (1295, 504), 0.72))
+# Foreground swinging greaves last, so they visibly occlude the planted legs.
+base.alpha_composite(warp(original, clean_greave, (213, 442), (224, 504), (821, 438), (931, 504), 0.78))
+base.alpha_composite(warp(original, clean_greave, (213, 442), (224, 504), (1270, 438), (1137, 504), 0.78))
+
+# Enforce the requested exact chroma-key background without touching armor.
+pixels = list(base.getdata())
+pixels = [(0, 255, 0, 255) if is_green(p) else p for p in pixels]
+base.putdata(pixels)
+base.convert("RGB").save(OUT, format="PNG")
+print(OUT)

+    dl = hypot(dx, dy)
+    # Source/destination orthonormal bases. Length changes along the shin,
+    # while width_scale keeps the greave from becoming unnaturally thick.
+    sux, suy = sx / sl, sy / sl
+    snx, sny = -suy, sux
+    dux, duy = dx / dl, dy / dl
+    dnx, dny = -duy * width_scale, dux * width_scale
+    along = dl / sl
+    # A = D * S^T because S is orthonormal.
+    a = dux * along * sux + dnx * snx
+    b = dux * along * suy + dnx * sny
+    d = duy * along * sux + dny * snx

+            pixel = ip[x, y]
+            if is_green(pixel):
+                rp[x, y] = 0
+                continue
+            if neutral_only:
+                r, g, b, _ = pixel
+                hi, lo = max(r, g, b), min(r, g, b)
+                # Greaves are neutral/warm silver plus near-black ink edges.
+                # This rejects the saturated blue cloak and brown hammer when
+                # erasing the old legs.
+                neutral_armor = hi - lo <= max(8, int(hi * 0.23))
+                if not neutral_armor:
+                    rp[x, y] = 0
+    return region
+
+
+def affine_from_segments(source_a, source_b, dest_a, dest_b, width_scale=1.0):
+    sx, sy = source_b[0] - source_a[0], source_b[1] - source_a[1]
+    dx, dy = dest_b[0] - dest_a[0], dest_b[1] - dest_a[1]
+    sl = hypot(sx, sy)
+    dl = hypot(dx, dy)
+    # Source/destination orthonormal bases. Length changes along the shin,
+    # while width_scale keeps the greave from becoming unnaturally thick.
+    sux, suy = sx / sl, sy / sl
+    snx, sny = -suy, sux
+    dux, duy = dx / dl, dy / dl
+    dnx, dny = -duy * width_scale, dux * width_scale
+    along = dl / sl
+    # A = D * S^T because S is orthonormal.
+    a = dux * along * sux + dnx * snx
+    b = dux * along * suy + dnx * sny
+    d = duy * along * sux + dny * snx
+    e = duy * along * suy + dny * sny
+    tx = dest_a[0] - a * source_a[0] - b * source_a[1]
+    ty = dest_a[1] - d * source_a[0] - e * source_a[1]
+    det = a * e - b * d
+    ia, ib = e / det, -b / det
+    id_, ie = -d / det, a / det
+    ic = -(ia * tx + ib * ty)
+    iff = -(id_ * tx + ie * ty)
+    return ia, ib, ic, id_, ie, iff
+
+
+def warp(image, mask, source_a, source_b, dest_a, dest_b, width_scale=1.0):
+    layer = Image.new("RGBA", image.size, (0, 0, 0, 0))
+    layer.paste(image, (0, 0), mask)
+    coeff = affine_from_segments(source_a, source_b, dest_a, dest_b, width_scale)
+    return layer.transform(
+        image.size,
+        Image.Transform.AFFINE,
+        coeff,
+        resample=Image.Resampling.BICUBIC,
+    )
+
+
+original = Image.open(SRC).convert("RGBA")
+base = original.copy()
+
+# Tight masks around the four original greaves only. Boots, knees, skirt,
+# hammer, and shield remain in the unmodified base layer.
+f3_swing = make_mask(original, [(804, 424), (843, 423), (849, 448), (831, 477), (818, 508), (785, 509), (791, 478), 
(803, 449)])
+f3_plant = make_mask(original, [(902, 442), (932, 438), (942, 463), (953, 506), (919, 509), (909, 480)])
+f4_swing = make_mask(original, [(1136, 424), (1177, 421), (1187, 449), (1169, 478), (1154, 508), (1118, 509), (1124, 
478), (1136, 449)])
+f4_plant = make_mask(original, [(1255, 439), (1285, 438), (1293, 464), (1308, 507), (1275, 509), (1264, 480)])
+clean_greave = make_mask(original, [(197, 431), (227, 430), (234, 466), (240, 507), (205, 509), (201, 474)], 
neutral_only=False)
+
+for mask in (f3_swing, f3_plant, f4_swing, f4_plant):
+    base.paste((0, 255, 0, 255), (0, 0), mask)
+
+# Background planted greaves first.
+base.alpha_composite(warp(original, clean_greave, (213, 442), (224, 504), (909, 442), (805, 504), 0.72))
+base.alpha_composite(warp(original, clean_greave, (213, 442), (224, 504), (1168, 442), (1295, 504), 0.72))
+# Foreground swinging greaves last, so they visibly occlude the planted legs.
+base.alpha_composite(warp(original, clean_greave, (213, 442), (224, 504), (821, 438), (931, 504), 0.78))
+base.alpha_composite(warp(original, clean_greave, (213, 442), (224, 504), (1270, 438), (1137, 504), 0.78))
+
+# Enforce the requested exact chroma-key background without touching armor.
+pixels = list(base.getdata())
+pixels = [(0, 255, 0, 255) if is_green(p) else p for p in pixels]
+base.putdata(pixels)
+base.convert("RGB").save(OUT, format="PNG")
+print(OUT)

+from math import hypot
+
+from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
+
+
+SRC = r"C:\Users\asali\.codex\generated_images\01a05455-f941-7b83-9f83-a6b71451313d\exec-d7d3aebe-85ce-45db-818b-893de
a4b7509.png"
+OUT = r"C:\Users\asali\.codex\generated_images\01a05455-f941-7b83-9f83-a6b71451313d\warp_test.png"
+
+
+def is_green(pixel):
+    r, g, b, _ = pixel
+    return g >= 80 and g >= r + 18 and g >= b + 18
+
+
+def make_mask(image, polygon, neutral_only=True):
+    region = Image.new("L", image.size, 0)
+    ImageDraw.Draw(region).polygon(polygon, fill=255)
+    rp = region.load()
+    ip = image.load()
+    xs = [p[0] for p in polygon]
+    ys = [p[1] for p in polygon]
+    for y in range(min(ys), max(ys) + 1):
+        for x in range(min(xs), max(xs) + 1):
+            if not rp[x, y]:
+                continue
+            pixel = ip[x, y]
+            if is_green(pixel):
+                rp[x, y] = 0
+                continue
+            if neutral_only:
+                r, g, b, _ = pixel
+                hi, lo = max(r, g, b), min(r, g, b)
+                # Greaves are neutral/warm silver plus near-black ink edges.
+                # This rejects the saturated blue cloak and brown hammer when
+                # erasing the old legs.
+                neutral_armor = hi - lo <= max(8, int(hi * 0.23))
+                if not neutral_armor:
+                    rp[x, y] = 0
+    return region
+
+
+def affine_from_segments(source_a, source_b, dest_a, dest_b, width_scale=1.0):
+    sx, sy = source_b[0] - source_a[0], source_b[1] - source_a[1]
+    dx, dy = dest_b[0] - dest_a[0], dest_b[1] - dest_a[1]
+    sl = hypot(sx, sy)
+    dl = hypot(dx, dy)
+    # Source/destination orthonormal bases. Length changes along the shin,
+    # while width_scale keeps the greave from becoming unnaturally thick.
+    sux, suy = sx / sl, sy / sl
+    snx, sny = -suy, sux
+    dux, duy = dx / dl, dy / dl
+    dnx, dny = -duy * width_scale, dux * width_scale
+    along = dl / sl
+    # A = D * S^T because S is orthonormal.
+    a = dux * along * sux + dnx * snx
+    b = dux * along * suy + dnx * sny
+    d = duy * along * sux + dny * snx
+    e = duy * along * suy + dny * sny
+    tx = dest_a[0] - a * source_a[0] - b * source_a[1]
+    ty = dest_a[1] - d * source_a[0] - e * source_a[1]

+    id_, ie = -d / det, a / det
+    ic = -(ia * tx + ib * ty)

+    return ia, ib, ic, id_, ie, iff

+    return layer.transform(
+        image.size,
+        Image.Transform.AFFINE,
+        coeff,
+        resample=Image.Resampling.BICUBIC,
+    )
+

+base = original.copy()
+
+# Tight masks around the four original greaves only. Boots, knees, skirt,
+# hammer, and shield remain in the unmodified base layer.

+    base.paste((0, 255, 0, 255), (0, 0), mask)

+# Background planted greaves first.
+base.alpha_composite(warp(original, clean_greave, (218, 456), (225, 504), (909, 442), (805, 504), 0.60))
+base.alpha_composite(warp(original, clean_greave, (218, 456), (225, 504), (1168, 442), (1295, 504), 0.60))
+# Foreground swinging greaves last, so they visibly occlude the planted legs.
+base.alpha_composite(warp(original, clean_greave, (218, 456), (225, 504), (821, 438), (931, 504), 0.67))
+base.alpha_composite(warp(original, clean_greave, (218, 456), (225, 504), (1270, 438), (1137, 504), 0.67))
+
+# Enforce the requested exact chroma-key background without touching armor.
+pixels = list(base.getdata())
+pixels = [(0, 255, 0, 255) if is_green(p) else p for p in pixels]
+base.putdata(pixels)
+base.convert("RGB").save(OUT, format="PNG")
+print(OUT)

+from math import hypot
+
+from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
+
+
+SRC = r"C:\Users\asali\.codex\generated_images\01a05455-f941-7b83-9f83-a6b71451313d\exec-d7d3aebe-85ce-45db-818b-893de
a4b7509.png"
+OUT = r"C:\Users\asali\.codex\generated_images\01a05455-f941-7b83-9f83-a6b71451313d\warp_test.png"
+
+
+def is_green(pixel):
+    r, g, b, _ = pixel
+    return g >= 80 and g >= r + 18 and g >= b + 18
+
+
+def make_mask(image, polygon, neutral_only=True):
+    region = Image.new("L", image.size, 0)
+    ImageDraw.Draw(region).polygon(polygon, fill=255)
+    rp = region.load()
+    ip = image.load()
+    xs = [p[0] for p in polygon]
+    ys = [p[1] for p in polygon]
+    for y in range(min(ys), max(ys) + 1):
+        for x in range(min(xs), max(xs) + 1):
+            if not rp[x, y]:
+                continue
+            pixel = ip[x, y]
+            if is_green(pixel):
+                rp[x, y] = 0
+                continue
+            if neutral_only:
+                r, g, b, _ = pixel
+                hi, lo = max(r, g, b), min(r, g, b)
+                # Greaves are neutral/warm silver plus near-black ink edges.
+                # This rejects the saturated blue cloak and brown hammer when
+                # erasing the old legs.
+                neutral_armor = hi - lo <= max(8, int(hi * 0.23))
+                if not neutral_armor:
+                    rp[x, y] = 0
+    return region
+
+
+def affine_from_segments(source_a, source_b, dest_a, dest_b, width_scale=1.0):
+    sx, sy = source_b[0] - source_a[0], source_b[1] - source_a[1]
+    dx, dy = dest_b[0] - dest_a[0], dest_b[1] - dest_a[1]
+    sl = hypot(sx, sy)
+    dl = hypot(dx, dy)
+    # Source/destination orthonormal bases. Length changes along the shin,
+    # while width_scale keeps the greave from becoming unnaturally thick.
+    sux, suy = sx / sl, sy / sl
+    snx, sny = -suy, sux
+    dux, duy = dx / dl, dy / dl
+    dnx, dny = -duy * width_scale, dux * width_scale
+    along = dl / sl
+    # A = D * S^T because S is orthonormal.
+    a = dux * along * sux + dnx * snx
+    b = dux * along * suy + dnx * sny
+    d = duy * along * sux + dny * snx
+    e = duy * along * suy + dny * sny
+    tx = dest_a[0] - a * source_a[0] - b * source_a[1]
+    ty = dest_a[1] - d * source_a[0] - e * source_a[1]
+    det = a * e - b * d
+    ia, ib = e / det, -b / det
+    id_, ie = -d / det, a / det
+    ic = -(ia * tx + ib * ty)
+    iff = -(id_ * tx + ie * ty)
+    return ia, ib, ic, id_, ie, iff
+
+
+def warp(image, mask, source_a, source_b, dest_a, dest_b, width_scale=1.0):
+    layer = Image.new("RGBA", image.size, (0, 0, 0, 0))
+    layer.paste(image, (0, 0), mask)
+    coeff = affine_from_segments(source_a, source_b, dest_a, dest_b, width_scale)
+    return layer.transform(
+        image.size,
+        Image.Transform.AFFINE,
+        coeff,
+        resample=Image.Resampling.BICUBIC,
+    )
+
+
+original = Image.open(SRC).convert("RGBA")
+base = original.copy()
+
+# Tight masks around the four original greaves only. Boots, knees, skirt,
+# hammer, and shield remain in the unmodified base layer.
+f3_swing = make_mask(original, [(804, 424), (843, 423), (849, 448), (831, 477), (818, 508), (785, 509), (791, 478), 
(803, 449)])
+f3_plant = make_mask(original, [(902, 442), (932, 438), (942, 463), (953, 506), (919, 509), (909, 480)])
+f4_swing = make_mask(original, [(1136, 424), (1177, 421), (1187, 449), (1169, 478), (1154, 508), (1118, 509), (1124, 
478), (1136, 449)])
+f4_plant = make_mask(original, [(1255, 439), (1285, 438), (1293, 464), (1308, 507), (1275, 509), (1264, 480)])
+clean_greave = make_mask(original, [(205, 451), (232, 450), (236, 476), (240, 507), (207, 509), (204, 476)], 
neutral_only=False)
+
+for mask in (f3_swing, f3_plant, f4_swing, f4_plant):
+    base.paste((0, 255, 0, 255), (0, 0), mask)
+
+# Background planted greaves first.
+base.alpha_composite(warp(original, clean_greave, (218, 456), (225, 504), (909, 442), (805, 504), 0.60))
+base.alpha_composite(warp(original, clean_greave, (218, 456), (225, 504), (1168, 442), (1295, 504), 0.60))
+# Foreground swinging greaves last, so they visibly occlude the planted legs.
+base.alpha_composite(warp(original, clean_greave, (218, 456), (225, 504), (821, 438), (931, 504), 0.67))
+base.alpha_composite(warp(original, clean_greave, (218, 456), (225, 504), (1270, 438), (1137, 504), 0.67))
+
+# Enforce the requested exact chroma-key background without touching armor.
+pixels = list(base.getdata())
+pixels = [(0, 255, 0, 255) if is_green(p) else p for p in pixels]
+base.putdata(pixels)
+base.convert("RGB").save(OUT, format="PNG")
+print(OUT)

+            if is_green(pixel):
+                rp[x, y] = 0
+                continue
+            if neutral_only:
+                r, g, b, _ = pixel
+                hi, lo = max(r, g, b), min(r, g, b)
+                # Greaves are neutral/warm silver plus near-black ink edges.
+                # This rejects the saturated blue cloak and brown hammer when
+                # erasing the old legs.
+                neutral_armor = hi - lo <= max(8, int(hi * 0.23))
+                if not neutral_armor:
+                    rp[x, y] = 0
+    return region
+
+
+def affine_from_segments(source_a, source_b, dest_a, dest_b, width_scale=1.0):
+    sx, sy = source_b[0] - source_a[0], source_b[1] - source_a[1]
+    dx, dy = dest_b[0] - dest_a[0], dest_b[1] - dest_a[1]
+    sl = hypot(sx, sy)
+    dl = hypot(dx, dy)
+    # Source/destination orthonormal bases. Length changes along the shin,
+    # while width_scale keeps the greave from becoming unnaturally thick.
+    sux, suy = sx / sl, sy / sl
+    snx, sny = -suy, sux
+    dux, duy = dx / dl, dy / dl
+    dnx, dny = -duy * width_scale, dux * width_scale
+    along = dl / sl
+    # A = D * S^T because S is orthonormal.
+    a = dux * along * sux + dnx * snx
+    b = dux * along * suy + dnx * sny
+    d = duy * along * sux + dny * snx
+    e = duy * along * suy + dny * sny
+    tx = dest_a[0] - a * source_a[0] - b * source_a[1]
+    ty = dest_a[1] - d * source_a[0] - e * source_a[1]
+    det = a * e - b * d
+    ia, ib = e / det, -b / det
+    id_, ie = -d / det, a / det
+    ic = -(ia * tx + ib * ty)
+    iff = -(id_ * tx + ie * ty)
+    return ia, ib, ic, id_, ie, iff
+
+
+def warp(image, mask, source_a, source_b, dest_a, dest_b, width_scale=1.0):
+    layer = Image.new("RGBA", image.size, (0, 0, 0, 0))
+    layer.paste(image, (0, 0), mask)
+    coeff = affine_from_segments(source_a, source_b, dest_a, dest_b, width_scale)
+    return layer.transform(
+        image.size,
+        Image.Transform.AFFINE,
+        coeff,
+        resample=Image.Resampling.BICUBIC,
+    )
+
+
+original = Image.open(SRC).convert("RGBA")
+base = original.copy()
+
+# Tight masks around the four original greaves only. Boots, knees, skirt,
+# hammer, and shield remain in the unmodified base layer.
+f3_swing = make_mask(original, [(804, 424), (843, 423), (849, 448), (831, 477), (818, 508), (785, 509), (791, 478), 
(803, 449)])
+f3_plant = make_mask(original, [(902, 442), (932, 438), (942, 463), (953, 506), (919, 509), (909, 480)])
+f4_swing = make_mask(original, [(1136, 424), (1177, 421), (1187, 449), (1169, 478), (1154, 508), (1118, 509), (1124, 
478), (1136, 449)])
+f4_plant = make_mask(original, [(1255, 439), (1285, 438), (1293, 464), (1308, 507), (1275, 509), (1264, 480)])
+clean_greave = make_mask(original, [(205, 451), (232, 450), (236, 476), (240, 507), (207, 509), (204, 476)], 
neutral_only=False)
+
+for mask in (f3_swing, f3_plant, f4_swing, f4_plant):
+    base.paste((0, 255, 0, 255), (0, 0), mask)
+
+# Background planted greaves first.
+base.alpha_composite(warp(original, clean_greave, (218, 456), (225, 504), (909, 442), (805, 504), 0.60))
+base.alpha_composite(warp(original, clean_greave, (218, 456), (225, 504), (1168, 442), (1295, 504), 0.60))
+# Foreground swinging greaves last, so they visibly occlude the planted legs.
+base.alpha_composite(warp(original, clean_greave, (218, 456), (225, 504), (821, 438), (931, 504), 0.67))
+base.alpha_composite(warp(original, clean_greave, (218, 456), (225, 504), (1270, 438), (1137, 504), 0.67))
+
+# Enforce the requested exact chroma-key background without touching armor.
+pixels = list(base.getdata())
+pixels = [(0, 255, 0, 255) if is_green(p) else p for p in pixels]
+base.putdata(pixels)
+base.convert("RGB").save(OUT, format="PNG")
+print(OUT)

diff --git a/art_src/stride_pilot_2026-08-27/paladin_e_v3/warp_legs.py 
b/art_src/stride_pilot_2026-08-27/paladin_e_v3/warp_legs.py

+++ b/art_src/stride_pilot_2026-08-27/paladin_e_v3/warp_legs.py
@@ -0,0 +1,107 @@
+from math import hypot
+
+from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
+
+
+SRC = r"C:\Users\asali\.codex\generated_images\01a05455-f941-7b83-9f83-a6b71451313d\exec-d7d3aebe-85ce-45db-818b-893de
a4b7509.png"
+OUT = r"C:\Users\asali\.codex\generated_images\01a05455-f941-7b83-9f83-a6b71451313d\warp_test.png"
+
+
+def is_green(pixel):
+    r, g, b, _ = pixel
+    return g >= 80 and g >= r + 18 and g >= b + 18
+
+
+def make_mask(image, polygon, neutral_only=True):
+    region = Image.new("L", image.size, 0)
+    ImageDraw.Draw(region).polygon(polygon, fill=255)
+    rp = region.load()
+    ip = image.load()
+    xs = [p[0] for p in polygon]
+    ys = [p[1] for p in polygon]
+    for y in range(min(ys), max(ys) + 1):
+        for x in range(min(xs), max(xs) + 1):
+            if not rp[x, y]:
+                continue
+            pixel = ip[x, y]
+            if is_green(pixel):
+                rp[x, y] = 0
+                continue
+            if neutral_only:
+                r, g, b, _ = pixel
+                hi, lo = max(r, g, b), min(r, g, b)
+                # Greaves are neutral/warm silver plus near-black ink edges.
+                # This rejects the saturated blue cloak and brown hammer when
+                # erasing the old legs.
+                neutral_armor = hi - lo <= max(8, int(hi * 0.23))
+                if not neutral_armor:
+                    rp[x, y] = 0
+    return region
+
+
+def affine_from_segments(source_a, source_b, dest_a, dest_b, width_scale=1.0):
+    sx, sy = source_b[0] - source_a[0], source_b[1] - source_a[1]
+    dx, dy = dest_b[0] - dest_a[0], dest_b[1] - dest_a[1]
+    sl = hypot(sx, sy)
+    dl = hypot(dx, dy)
+    # Source/destination orthonormal bases. Length changes along the shin,
+    # while width_scale keeps the greave from becoming unnaturally thick.
+    sux, suy = sx / sl, sy / sl
+    snx, sny = -suy, sux
+    dux, duy = dx / dl, dy / dl
+    dnx, dny = -duy * width_scale, dux * width_scale
+    along = dl / sl
+    # A = D * S^T because S is orthonormal.
+    a = dux * along * sux + dnx * snx
+    b = dux * along * suy + dnx * sny
+    d = duy * along * sux + dny * snx
+    e = duy * along * suy + dny * sny
+    tx = dest_a[0] - a * source_a[0] - b * source_a[1]
+    ty = dest_a[1] - d * source_a[0] - e * source_a[1]
+    det = a * e - b * d
+    ia, ib = e / det, -b / det
+    id_, ie = -d / det, a / det
+    ic = -(ia * tx + ib * ty)
+    iff = -(id_ * tx + ie * ty)
+    return ia, ib, ic, id_, ie, iff
+
+
+def warp(image, mask, source_a, source_b, dest_a, dest_b, width_scale=1.0):
+    layer = Image.new("RGBA", image.size, (0, 0, 0, 0))
+    layer.paste(image, (0, 0), mask)
+    coeff = affine_from_segments(source_a, source_b, dest_a, dest_b, width_scale)
+    return layer.transform(
+        image.size,
+        Image.Transform.AFFINE,
+        coeff,
+        resample=Image.Resampling.BICUBIC,
+    )
+
+
+original = Image.open(SRC).convert("RGBA")
+base = original.copy()
+
+# Tight masks around the four original greaves only. Boots, knees, skirt,
+# hammer, and shield remain in the unmodified base layer.
+f3_swing = make_mask(original, [(804, 424), (843, 423), (849, 448), (831, 477), (818, 508), (785, 509), (791, 478), 
(803, 449)])
+f3_plant = make_mask(original, [(902, 442), (932, 438), (942, 463), (953, 506), (919, 509), (909, 480)])
+f4_swing = make_mask(original, [(1136, 424), (1177, 421), (1187, 449), (1169, 478), (1154, 508), (1118, 509), (1124, 
478), (1136, 449)])
+f4_plant = make_mask(original, [(1255, 439), (1285, 438), (1293, 464), (1308, 507), (1275, 509), (1264, 480)])
+clean_greave = make_mask(original, [(212, 469), (232, 469), (238, 506), (208, 509)], neutral_only=False)
+
+for mask in (f3_swing, f3_plant, f4_swing, f4_plant):
+    base.paste((0, 255, 0, 255), (0, 0), mask)
+
+# Background planted greaves first.
+base.alpha_composite(warp(original, clean_greave, (222, 473), (224, 504), (909, 442), (805, 504), 0.60))
+base.alpha_composite(warp(original, clean_greave, (222, 473), (224, 504), (1168, 442), (1295, 504), 0.60))
+# Foreground swinging greaves last, so they visibly occlude the planted legs.
+base.alpha_composite(warp(original, clean_greave, (222, 473), (224, 504), (821, 438), (931, 504), 0.67))
+base.alpha_composite(warp(original, clean_greave, (222, 473), (224, 504), (1270, 438), (1137, 504), 0.67))
+
+# Enforce the requested exact chroma-key background without touching armor.
+pixels = list(base.getdata())
+pixels = [(0, 255, 0, 255) if is_green(p) else p for p in pixels]
+base.putdata(pixels)
+base.convert("RGB").save(OUT, format="PNG")
+print(OUT)
